DEFINE a2k_base_year         = 50 #                                     0000001300000200  
% Base year value for this system                                       0000001400000250  
                                                                        0000001500000300  
      ,a2k_century_19        = 19 #                                     0000001600000400  
% Century value for this century                                        0000001700000450  
                                                                        0000001800000500  
      ,a2k_century_20        = 20 #                                     0000001900000600  
% Century value for next century                                        0000002000000650  
                                                                        0000002100000700  
      ,a2k_amd_wt_aa         = 10000 #                                  0000002200000800  
      ,a2k_camd_wt_cc        = 1000000 #                                0000002300001000  
      ;                                                                 0000002400001200  
                                                                        0000002500001400  
INTEGER a2k_date_int_8                                                  0000002600001450  
% Temporary integer date variable for date manipulation                 0000002700001460  
       ;                                                                0000002800001500  
                                                                        0000002900001550  
EBCDIC ARRAY a2k_date_ebcdic_8 [0:7]                                    0000003000001600  
% Temporary ebcdic array of size 8 for date manipulation                0000003100001620  
            ,a2k_date_ebcdic_5 [0:4]                                    0000003200001622  
% Temporary ebcdic array of size 5 for julian date manipulation         0000003300001624  
            ,a2k_date_ebcdic_7 [0:6]                                    0000003400001630  
% Temporary ebcdic array of size 7 for julian date manipulation         0000003500001640  
            ;                                                           0000003600001650  
                                                                        0000003700001700

PROCEDURE a2k_conv_amd_to_camd_int (a2k_date_int_6                      0000004400002000  
                                   ,a2k_date_int_8                      0000004500004000  
                                   );                                   0000004600006000  
                                                                        0000004700008000  
% Procedure           :      a2k_conv_amd_to_camd_int                   0000004800010000  
%                                                                       0000004900014000  
% Input Parameter     :      Six digit date in AAMMDD format in an      0000005000016000  
%                            integer variable                           0000005100018000  
% Output parameter    :      Eight digit date in CCAAMMDD format in an  0000005200020000  
%                            intager variable                           0000005300022000  
%                                                                       0000005400024000  
% Description         :      This procedure accepts the six digit date  0000005500024200  
%                     :      AAMMDD format into an integer variable. It 0000005600024400  
%                     :      calculates the century value based on base 0000005700024600  
%                     :      year concept and returns the date in       0000005800024800  
%                     :      CCAAMMDD format in another integer variable0000005900025000  
                                                                        0000006000025200  
INTEGER a2k_date_int_6                                                  0000006100026000  
       ,a2k_date_int_8                                                  0000006200028000  
       ;                                                                0000006300030000  
                                                                        0000006400032000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000006500034000  
                                                                        0000006600036000  
    INTEGER a2k_year                                                    0000006700048000  
           ;                                                            0000006800050000  
                                                                        0000006900060000  
    % Compare the year field with the base year value                   0000007000062000  
    a2k_year := a2k_date_int_6 - a2k_base_year * a2k_amd_wt_aa ;        0000007100064000  
                                                                        0000007200074000  
    % Calculating the century based on base year concept                0000007300076000  
    IF a2k_year geq 0 THEN                                              0000007400078000  
        a2k_date_int_8 := a2k_date_int_6 + a2k_century_19               0000007500080000  
                          * a2k_camd_wt_cc                              0000007600080500  
    ELSE                                                                0000007700082000  
        a2k_date_int_8 := a2k_date_int_6 + a2k_century_20 *             0000007800084000  
                          a2k_camd_wt_cc ;                              0000007900090000  
    END;                                                                0000008000092000  
                                                                        0000008100092050  
PROCEDURE a2k_conv_amd_to_camd_ebcdic (a2k_date_six                     0000008200092100  
                                      ,a2k_date_eight                   0000008300092200  
                                      );                                0000008400092300  
                                                                        0000008500092400  
% Procedure           :      a2k_conv_amd_to_camd_ebcdic                0000008600092500  
%                                                                       0000008700092600  
% Input Parameter     :      Six digit date in AAMMDD format in an      0000008800092700  
%                            ebcdic array                               0000008900092800  
% Output parameter    :      Eight digit date in CCAAMMDD format in an  0000009000092900  
%                            ebcdic array                               0000009100093000  
%                                                                       0000009200093100  
% Description         :      This procedure accepts the six digit date  0000009300093200  
%                     :      AAMMDD format into an ebcdic array. It     0000009400093300  
%                     :      calculates the century value based on base 0000009500093400  
%                     :      year concept and returns the date in       0000009600093500  
%                     :      CCAAMMDD format in another ebcdic array of 0000009700093600  
%                     :      size 8 bytes.                              0000009800093700  
                                                                        0000009900093800  
                                                                        0000010000093900  
EBCDIC ARRAY a2k_date_six [0]                                           0000010100094000  
            ,a2k_date_eight [0]                                         0000010200094100  
            ;                                                           0000010300094200  
                                                                        0000010400094300  
    BEGIN                                                               0000010500094400  
                                                                        0000010600094500  
    INTEGER year                                                        0000010700095100  
           ;                                                            0000010800095200  
                                                                        0000010900095300  
    POINTER a2k_date_six_ptr                                            0000011000095400  
           ,a2k_date_eight_ptr                                          0000011100095500  
           ;                                                            0000011200095600  
                                                                        0000011300095700  
    % Separating out the year part from the six digit date field        0000011400095800  
    year := INTEGER (a2k_date_six[0],2);                                0000011500095900  
                                                                        0000011600096000  
    % Initialising pointers                                             0000011700096100  
    a2k_date_six_ptr := a2k_date_six [0] ;                              0000011800096200  
    a2k_date_eight_ptr := a2k_date_eight [0] ;                          0000011900096300  
                                                                        0000012000096400  
    % Calculating the century based on base year concept                0000012100096500  
    IF year geq a2k_base_year THEN                                      0000012200096600  
        REPLACE a2k_date_eight_ptr:a2k_date_eight_ptr BY                0000012300096700  
            STRING(a2k_century_19,*)                                    0000012400096750  
    ELSE                                                                0000012500096800  
        REPLACE a2k_date_eight_ptr:a2k_date_eight_ptr BY                0000012600096900  
            STRING(a2k_century_20,*);                                   0000012700096950  
                                                                        0000012800097000  
    REPLACE a2k_date_eight_ptr BY a2k_date_six_ptr for                  0000012900097100  
        size(a2k_date_six);                                             0000013000097150  
                                                                        0000013100097200  
    END;                                                                0000013200097300  
                                                                        0000013300098000  
PROCEDURE a2k_conv_camd_to_amd_int (a2k_date_int_8                      0000013400100000  
                                   ,a2k_date_int_6                      0000013500102000  
                                   );                                   0000013600104000  
                                                                        0000013700106000  
% Procedure           :      a2k_conv_camd_to_amd_int                   0000013800108000  
%                                                                       0000013900110000  
% Input Parameter     :      Eight digit date in CCAAMMDD format in an  0000014000112000  
%                            integer variable                           0000014100114000  
% Output parameter    :      Six digit date in AAMMDD format in an      0000014200116000  
%                            intager variable                           0000014300118000  
%                                                                       0000014400120000  
% Description         :      This procedure accepts the eight digit date0000014500122000  
%                     :      CCAAMMDD format into an integer variable.It0000014600124000  
%                     :      truncates the century value and returns the0000014700126000  
%                     :      date in AAMMDD format in another integer   0000014800128000  
%                     :      variable.                                  0000014900130000  
                                                                        0000015000132000  
INTEGER a2k_date_int_8                                                  0000015100134000  
       ,a2k_date_int_6                                                  0000015200136000  
       ;                                                                0000015300138000  
                                                                        0000015400140000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000015500142000  
                                                                        0000015600144000  
    % Truncating the century field from 8 digit date to make it a six   0000015700146000  
    % digit date                                                        0000015800148000  
    a2k_date_int_6 := a2k_date_int_8 MOD a2k_camd_wt_cc;                0000015900150000  
                                                                        0000016000152000  
    END;                                                                0000016100154000  
                                                                        0000016200156000  
PROCEDURE a2k_conv_ad_to_cad_ebcdic (a2k_date_five                      0000016300158000  
                                    ,a2k_date_seven                     0000016400160000  
                                    );                                  0000016500162000  
                                                                        0000016600164000  
% Procedure           :      a2k_conv_ad_to_cad_ebcdic                  0000016700166000  
%                                                                       0000016800168000  
% Input Parameter     :      Five digit date in AADDD julian format in  0000016900170000  
%                            an ebcdic array                            0000017000172000  
% Output parameter    :      Seven digit date in CCAADDD format in an   0000017100174000  
%                            ebcdic array                               0000017200176000  
%                                                                       0000017300178000  
% Description         :      This procedure accepts the five digit date 0000017400180000  
%                     :      AADDD julian format into an ebcdic array.It0000017500182000  
%                     :      calculates the century value based on base 0000017600184000  
%                     :      year concept and returns the date in julian0000017700186000  
%                     :      CCAADDD format in another ebcdic array of  0000017800188000  
%                     :      size 7 bytes.                              0000017900190000  
                                                                        0000018000192000  
                                                                        0000018100194000  
EBCDIC ARRAY a2k_date_five [0]                                          0000018200196000  
            ,a2k_date_seven [0]                                         0000018300198000  
            ;                                                           0000018400200000  
                                                                        0000018500202000  
    BEGIN                                                               0000018600204000  
                                                                        0000018700206000  
    INTEGER year                                                        0000018800208000  
           ;                                                            0000018900210000  
                                                                        0000019000212000  
    POINTER a2k_date_five_ptr                                           0000019100214000  
           ,a2k_date_seven_ptr                                          0000019200216000  
           ;                                                            0000019300218000  
                                                                        0000019400220000  
    % Separating out the year part from the five digit date field       0000019500222000  
    year := INTEGER (a2k_date_five[0],2);                               0000019600224000  
                                                                        0000019700226000  
    % Initialising pointers                                             0000019800228000  
    a2k_date_five_ptr := a2k_date_five [0] ;                            0000019900230000  
    a2k_date_seven_ptr := a2k_date_seven [0] ;                          0000020000232000  
                                                                        0000020100234000  
    % Calculating the century based on base year concept                0000020200236000  
    IF year geq a2k_base_year THEN                                      0000020300238000  
        REPLACE a2k_date_seven_ptr:a2k_date_seven_ptr BY                0000020400240000  
            STRING(a2k_century_19,*)                                    0000020500242000  
    ELSE                                                                0000020600244000  
        REPLACE a2k_date_seven_ptr:a2k_date_seven_ptr BY                0000020700246000  
            STRING(a2k_century_20,*);                                   0000020800248000  
                                                                        0000020900250000  
    REPLACE a2k_date_seven_ptr BY a2k_date_five_ptr for                 0000021000252000  
        size(a2k_date_five);                                            0000021100254000  
                                                                        0000021200256000  
    END;                                                                0000021300258000  
                                                                        0000021400258100  
                                                                        0000021500258200  
PROCEDURE a2k_conv_amd_to_camd_hex (a2k_date_six                        0000021600258300  
                                   ,a2k_date_eight                      0000021700258400  
                                   );                                   0000021800258500  
                                                                        0000021900258600  
% Procedure           :      a2k_conv_amd_to_camd_hex                   0000022000258700  
%                                                                       0000022100258800  
% Input Parameter     :      Six digit date in AAMMDD format in an      0000022200258900  
%                            hex array                                  0000022300259000  
% Output parameter    :      Eight digit date in CCAAMMDD format in an  0000022400259100  
%                            hex array                                  0000022500259200  
%                                                                       0000022600259300  
% Description         :      This procedure accepts the six digit date  0000022700259400  
%                     :      AAMMDD format into an hex array.           0000022800259500  
%                     :      It calculates the century value based on   0000022900259600  
%                     :      base year concept and returns the date in  0000023000259700  
%                     :      CCAAMMDD format in another hex array .     0000023100259800  
                                                                        0000023200260000  
                                                                        0000023300260100  
HEX ARRAY a2k_date_six [0]                                              0000023400260200  
         ,a2k_date_eight [0]                                            0000023500260300  
         ;                                                              0000023600260400  
                                                                        0000023700260500  
    BEGIN                                                               0000023800260600  
                                                                        0000023900260700  
    INTEGER year                                                        0000024000260800  
           ;                                                            0000024100260900  
                                                                        0000024200261000  
    POINTER a2k_date_six_ptr                                            0000024300261100  
           ,a2k_date_eight_ptr                                          0000024400261200  
           ;                                                            0000024500261300  
                                                                        0000024600261400  
    % Separating out the year part from the six digit date field        0000024700261500  
    year := INTEGER (a2k_date_six[0],2);                                0000024800261600  
                                                                        0000024900261700  
    % Initialising pointers                                             0000025000261800  
    a2k_date_six_ptr := a2k_date_six [0] ;                              0000025100261900  
    a2k_date_eight_ptr := a2k_date_eight [0] ;                          0000025200262000  
                                                                        0000025300262100  
    % Calculating the century based on base year concept                0000025400262200  
    IF year geq a2k_base_year THEN                                      0000025500262300  
        REPLACE a2k_date_eight_ptr:a2k_date_eight_ptr BY                0000025600262400  
            a2k_century_19 FOR 2 DIGITS                                 0000025700262500  
    ELSE                                                                0000025800262600  
        REPLACE a2k_date_eight_ptr:a2k_date_eight_ptr BY                0000025900262700  
            a2k_century_20 FOR 2 DIGITS;                                0000026000262800  
                                                                        0000026100262900  
    REPLACE a2k_date_eight_ptr BY a2k_date_six_ptr for                  0000026200263000  
        size(a2k_date_six);                                             0000026300263100  
                                                                        0000026400263200  
    END;                                                                0000026500263300

  GRABALOG(LOG,ALOG,NIVLOG,FECDIA,BLOG,DES,NIVDES)=                     0000060600292002  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000060700293002  
    IF NIVLOG = 0 THEN                                                  0000060800294002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000060900295002  
      REPLACE POINTER(BLOG) BY "HD",                                    0000061000296002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000061100298002  
                               FECDIA  FOR 8 DIGITS,                    0000061200299002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000061300300002  
                               0       FOR 8 DIGITS,                    0000061400301002  
                               0       FOR 2 DIGITS;                    0000061500302002  
      WRITE(LOG[0],75,BLOG);                                            0000061600303002  
      LAST_RLOG[NUMDIAC1]:=LOG.LASTRECORD;                              0000061700304002  
    END;                                                                0000061800305002  
    REPLACE DFAUT(ALOG) BY (NIVLOG+1) FOR 8 DIGITS;                     0000061900306002  
    REPLACE DFAUTFAN(ALOG2) BY (NIVLOG+1) FOR 8 DIGITS;                 0000062000306102  
    IF IDFFUNCION(ALOG)  = 1 THEN                                       0000062100307002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000062200321002  
       IF NIVDES = 0 THEN                                               0000062300322002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000062400323002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000062500325002  
         REPLACE POINTER(BLOG) BY "HD",FECDIA FOR 8 DIGITS;             0000062600326002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000062700327002  
         WRITE(DES[0],90,BLOG);                                         0000062800328002  
         LAST_RDES[NUMDIAC1]:=DES.LASTRECORD;                           0000062900329002  
         REPLACE POINTER(BLOG) BY "HD",                                 0000063000332002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000063100334002  
                                  FECDIA FOR   8 DIGITS,                0000063200335002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000063300336002  
                                  NIVLOG FOR   8 DIGITS,                0000063400337002  
                                  1      FOR   2 DIGITS,                0000063500338002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000063600340002  
                                  40"0"  FOR 878 ;                      0000063700341002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000063800342002  
         WRITE(LOG[0],75,BLOG);                                         0000063900343002  
       END;                                                             0000064000344002  
       APUN_DESC:= 0;                                                   0000064100344102  
       P_ALOG := POINTER(ALOG)+445;                                     0000064200346002  
       SCAN P_ALOG         FOR LEN_REG:530 UNTIL NEQ 48"00";            0000064300347002  
       IF  LEN_REG > 0 THEN                                             0000064400348002  
          BEGIN                                                         0000064500350002  
             REPLACE POINTER(BLOG,4) BY (NIVLOG +1) FOR 8 DIGITS,       0000064600351002  
                                POINTER(ALOG,4)+890 FOR 1060,           0000064700352002  
                                                  0 FOR 10 DIGITS,      0000064800353002  
                                                 01 FOR  2 DIGITS;      0000064900353102  
             WRITE(DES[NIVDES+1],90,BLOG);                              0000065000354002  
             NIVDES:= * + 1;                                            0000065100355002  
             APUN_DESC:= NIVDES;                                        0000065200355102  
          END;                                                          0000065300357002  
                                                                        0000065400358002  
       REPLACE POINTER(BLOG,4) BY DFFUNCION(ALOG)   FOR 890,            0000065500379002  
                               APUN_DESC            FOR 8   DIGITS,     0000065600380002  
                               1                    FOR 2   DIGITS %STA 0000065700382002  
                              ;                                         0000065800383002  
    END                                                                 0000065900384002  
    ELSE                                                                0000066000385002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000066100385102  
       REPLACE DFINDATADI(ALOG) BY 0 FOR 2 DIGITS;                      0000066200385202  
       REPLACE POINTER(BLOG,4) BY DFFUNCION(ALOG) FOR 890,              0000066300386002  
                                                0 FOR 8   DIGITS,       0000066400387002  
                                                1 FOR 2   DIGITS %STA   0000066500389002  
                                 ;                                      0000066600390002  
    END;                                                                0000066700390102  
    WRITE(LOG[NIVLOG + 1],75,BLOG);                                     0000066800391002  
    NIVLOG:= * + 1;                                                     0000066900392002  
  END#,                                                                 0000067000393002

  DFELIMINA(LOGO,ALOG,NIVLOG,FECDIA,BLOG,NIVDES)=                       0000067300396002  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000067400397002  
    IF IDFAUTELIM(ALOG) > NIVLOG THEN                                   0000067500398002  
       RESULT:= 15                                                      0000067600399002  
    ELSE                                                                0000067700400002  
    IF READ(LOGO[IDFAUTELIM(ALOG)],75,BLOG) THEN                        0000067800401002  
       RESULT:= 9                                                       0000067900402002  
    ELSE                                                                0000068000403002  
    IF IDFSISTEMA(ALOG) NEQ IDFSISTEMA(BLOG) THEN                       0000068100404002  
       RESULT:= 9                                                       0000068200405002  
    ELSE                                                                0000068300406002  
    IF IDFFUNCION(BLOG) NEQ 1 THEN                                      0000068400407002  
       RESULT:= 11                                                      0000068500408002  
    ELSE                                                                0000068600409002  
    IF IDFAUTELIM(ALOG) = 0 THEN                                        0000068700410002  
       RESULT:= 12                                                      0000068800411002  
    ELSE                                                                0000068900412002  
    IF IDFSTATUS(BLOG) = 2 THEN                                         0000069000413002  
       RESULT:= 13                                                      0000069100414002  
    ELSE                                                                0000069200415002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000069300416002  
      REPLACE DFSTATUS(BLOG) BY 02 FOR 2 DIGITS;                        0000069400417002  
      REPLACE POINTER(ALOG2,4)+358 BY 2 FOR 2 DIGITS;                   0000069500417102  
      WRITE(LOGO[IDFAUTELIM(ALOG)],75,BLOG);                            0000069600418002  
      WHILE IDFAUTANT(BLOG) NEQ 0 DO                                    0000069700419002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000069800420002  
         AUT_ANTER:=IDFAUTANT(BLOG);                                    0000069900421002  
         READ (LOGO[AUT_ANTER],75,BLOG);                                0000070000422002  
         REPLACE DFSTATUS(BLOG) BY 02 FOR 2 DIGITS;                     0000070100423002  
         WRITE(LOGO[AUT_ANTER],75,BLOG);                                0000070200424002  
         IF AUT_ANTER = IDFAUTANT(BLOG) THEN                            0000070300425002  
            REPLACE DFAUTANT(BLOG) BY 00 FOR 2 DIGITS;                  0000070400426002  
      END;                                                              0000070500427002  
      REPLACE DFAUT(ALOG) BY (NIVLOG+1) FOR 8 DIGITS;                   0000070600428002  
      REPLACE DFAUTFAN(ALOG2) BY (NIVLOG+1) FOR 8 DIGITS;               0000070700428102  
      REPLACE POINTER(ALOG,4)+890  BY 0  FOR 8   DIGITS,                0000070800429002  
                                      1  FOR 2   DIGITS;                0000070900429102  
      WRITE(LOGO[NIVLOG + 1],75,ALOG);                                  0000071000430002  
      NIVLOG:= * + 1;                                                   0000071100431002  
    END;                                                                0000071200432002  
  END#,                                                                 0000071300433002  
                                                                        0000071400434002

  BORRAXPROC(LOGO,ALOG,FECDIA)=                                         0000074600461002  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000074700462002  
     NUM_REG:=0;                                                        0000074800463002  
     REG_LEIDOS:=-1;                                                    0000074900464002  
     CLOSE (LOGO,LOCK);                                                 0000075000465002  
     LOGO.BLOCKSIZE :=10800;                                            0000075100466002  
                                                                        0000075200467002  
     WHILE NOT READ(LOGO[REG_LEIDOS:= * + 1],75,ALOG) AND               0000075300468002  
            INTEGER(POINTER(ALOG,4),2) NEQ 99 DO                        0000075400469002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000075500470002  
       IF     IDFFUNCION(ALOG)  = 1                AND                  0000075600471002  
%             IDFSISTEMA(ALOG)  = IDFSISTEMA(ALOG) AND                  0000075700472002  
              IDFTIPPROC(ALOG)  = PROCAELIM        AND                  0000075800473002  
              IDFSTATUS (ALOG)  = 1               THEN                  0000075900474002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000076000475002  
            NUM_REG:= * + 1;                                            0000076100476002  
            REPLACE POINTER(ALOG,4)+ 899 BY 3 FOR 1 DIGITS;             0000076200477002  
            WRITE(LOGO[REG_LEIDOS],75,ALOG);                            0000076300478002  
       END;                                                             0000076400479002  
     END;                                                               0000076500480002  
     CLOSE (LOGO,LOCK);                                                 0000076600481002  
     LOGO.BLOCKSIZE :=150;                                              0000076700482002  
%    REPLACE   BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ);                                  0000076800483002  
%    REPLACE MSGLJ  BY "S151/L010/001:I>050110<";                       0000076900484002  
%    REPLACE TXTMSG BY "REG.ELIM.X PROC. ",                             0000077000485002  
%                      PROCAELIM  FOR * DIGITS, " CON FECHA ",          0000077100486002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000077200488002

%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000077400490002

  DFAMPLIAFILE(FILE_AMP,LEV_INI,ADD_REC,ARR_VACIO,REG_SIZE)=            0000083200550002  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000083300551002  
     ERRORMSG;                                                          0000083400552002  
     REPLACE POINTER(ARR_VACIO,4) BY 99 FOR 2 DIGITS;                   0000083500557002  
     CLOSE(FILE_AMP,LOCK) ;                                             0000083600558002  
     BLOCK_SIZE := FILE_AMP.BLOCKSIZE;                                  0000083700559002  
     IF BLOCK_SIZE = 150 THEN                                           0000083800559102  
        FILE_AMP.SYNCHRONIZE := VALUE (NO);                             0000083900559202  
     FILE_AMP.BLOCKSIZE :=10800 ;                                       0000084000560002  
     FOR R:= LEV_INI STEP 1 UNTIL (LEV_INI + ADD_REC) DO                0000084100561002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000084200562002  
         WRITE (FILE_AMP[R],REG_SIZE,ARR_VACIO);                        0000084300563002  
     END;                                                               0000084400564002  
     CLOSE(FILE_AMP,LOCK);                                              0000084500565002  
     IF BLOCK_SIZE = 150 THEN                                           0000084600565102  
        FILE_AMP.SYNCHRONIZE := VALUE (OUT);                            0000084700565202  
     FILE_AMP.BLOCKSIZE := BLOCK_SIZE;                                  0000084800566002  
  END#,                                                                 0000084900574002  
%-------------------------------------------------------------          0000085000575002  
     ERRORMSG = BEGIN                                                   0000085100576002  
                  REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ);                 0000085200577002  
                  REPLACE MSGLJ BY "S151/L002/050:I>050110<";           0000085300578002  
                END#,                                                   0000085400579002  
     HDRMSG  = "S151/L002/051:" #,                                      0000085500580002  
     ERRORDB = BEGIN                                                    0000085600581002  
               REPLACE MSGLJ       BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ);              0000085700582002  
               REPLACE MSGLJ       BY HDRMSG,"E>04",                    0000085800583002  
                       DMERR.DMERROR     FOR 2 DIGITS,                  0000085900584002  
                       DMERR.DMERRORTYPE FOR 2 DIGITS,                  0000086000585002  
                       "< LIBRARY REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,     0000086100586002  
                       " ERROR EN DMSII ",                              0000086200587002  
                       "ESTRUCTURA ",DMERR.DMSTRUCTURE FOR 2 DIGITS;    0000086300588002  
                       LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                 0000086400589002  
               END#,                                                    0000086500590002  
                                                                        0000086600591002

     TIEMPO = 3#,                                                       0000120301113002  
     ACQUIREL(E) =                                                      0000120401114002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000120501115002  
        TOTAL := 0;                                                     0000120601116002  
        RES_LOCK := LOCK(E,TIEMPO);                                     0000120701117002  
        WHILE RES_LOCK > 1 DO                                           0000120801118002  
        BEGIN                                                           0000120901119002  
           TOTAL := * + TIEMPO;                                         0000121001120002  
           IF TOTAL > 15 THEN                                           0000121101121002  
           BEGIN                                                        0000121201122002  
              MIX_LOCK := LOCKSTATUS(E).[47:24];                        0000121301123002  
              ERRORMSG;                                                 0000121401124002  
              REPLACE TXTMSG BY "Llevo "                    ,           0000121501125002  
                                TOTAL FOR 4 DIGITS          ,           0000121601126002  
                                " Seg en espera del Lock,"  ,           0000121701127002  
                                " La mezcla "               ,           0000121801128002  
                                MIX_LOCK FOR 4 DIGITS       ,           0000121901129002  
                                " Lo tiene tomado "         ;           0000122001130002  
              LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                          0000122101131002  
           END;                                                         0000122201132002  
           RES_LOCK := LOCK(E,TIEMPO);                                  0000122301133002  
        END;                                                            0000122401134002  
     END#,                                                              0000122501135002

     DFVALIDA(ALOG,SISTEMA,PRODUCTO,GIND)=                              0000123601146002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000123701147002  
       RESULT:=0;                                                       0000123801148002  
       IF IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,1) = 0 THEN                                   0000123901149002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000124001150002  
          ERRORMSG;                                                     0000124101151002  
%         REPLACE TXTMSG BY "CVE ERRONEA = ", NUM_CLAVE FOR 4 DIGITS,   0000124201152002  
%                           ", INDICE  1" ;                             0000124301153002  
%         LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                              0000124401154002  
          RESULT := 3;                                                  0000124501155002  
       END;                                                             0000124601156002  
       IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000124701157002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000124801158002  
          GIND:= 0;                                                     0000124901159002  
          WHILE GIND:= * + 1          LEQ 5 AND                         0000125001160002  
                IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,GIND)   > 0 DO                          0000125101161002  
            BEGIN                                                       0000125201162002  
                NUM_CLAVE := IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,GIND);                     0000125301163002  
                IF IADFCVECOL(NUM_CLAVE) LEQ 0 THEN                     0000125401164002  
                BEGIN                                                   0000125501165002  
                   ERRORMSG;                                            0000125601166002  
%                  REPLACE TXTMSG BY "CVE ERRONEA = ",                  0000125701167002  
%                                    NUM_CLAVE FOR 4 DIGITS ,           0000125801168002  
%                                    ", INDICE ",GIND FOR 2 DIGITS;     0000125901169002  
%                  LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                     0000126001170002  
                   RESULT := 3;                                         0000126101171002  
                END;                                                    0000126201172002  
            END;                                                        0000126301173002  
       END;                                                             0000126401174002  
       IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000126501175002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000126601176002  
          GIND:= 0;                                                     0000126701177002  
          WHILE GIND:= * + 1          LEQ 5 AND                         0000126801178002  
                IDFIMPOCUR(ALOG,GIND)   > 0 DO                          0000126901179002  
            BEGIN                                                       0000127001180002  
                NUM_CLAVE := IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG,GIND);                     0000127101181002  
                                                                        0000127201182002  
                IF (IADFCVEINDIC(NUM_CLAVE) = 2  OR                     0000127301183002  
                    IADFCVEINDIC(NUM_CLAVE) = 3) AND                    0000127401184002  
                    IDFSUCPROM(ALOG)            LEQ  0  THEN            0000127501185002  
                    RESULT := 5;                                        0000127601186002  
                IF (IADFCVEINDIC(NUM_CLAVE) = 1  OR                     0000127701187002  
                    IADFCVEINDIC(NUM_CLAVE) = 3) AND                    0000127801188002  
                    IDFSUCINI(ALOG)              LEQ 0  THEN            0000127901189002  
                    RESULT := 6;                                        0000128001190002  
                                                                        0000128101191002  
            END;                                                        0000128201192002  
       END;                                                             0000128301193002  
       IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000128401194002  
       IF IDFORIGEN(ALOG) = 1 OR                                        0000128501195002  
          IDFORIGEN(ALOG) = 2 OR                                        0000128601196002  
          IDFORIGEN(ALOG) = 3 THEN                                      0000128701197002  
       ELSE                                                             0000128801198002  
          RESULT:= 10;                                                  0000128901199002  
                                                                        0000129001200002  
       IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                               0000129101201002  
          IF IDFMONEDA(ALOG) > 0 THEN                                   0000129201202002  
          ELSE RESULT := 14;                                            0000129301203002  
                                                                        0000129401204002  
     END#,                                                              0000129501205002

 BOOLEAN PROCEDURE EXTERNO_P015;                                        0000193101778002  
%*******************************************************************    0000193201779002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000193301780002  
    STAT_P015[NUMDIAC1].STATUS := VALUE(NEVERUSED);                     0000193401781002  
    REPLACE NOM_ASINC BY "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P015/ASINCS",               0000193501782002  
                           SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,                       0000193601783002  
                   FEC_DIA[NUMDIAC1] FOR 6 DIGITS,                      0000193701784002  
                           "/",                                         0000193801785002  
                           MTP_P015 FOR 8,                              0000193901786002  
                           " ON CMEMP.";                                0000194001787002  
                                                                        0000194101788002  
    REPLACE STAT_P015[NUMDIAC1].NAME BY NOM_ASINC;                      0000194201789002  
%   REPLACE PARAM BY FEC_DIA[NUMDIAC1] FOR 6 DIGITS ,                   0000194301790002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000194401791002  
    REPLACE PARAM BY FEC_DIA[NUMDIAC1] FOR 8 DIGITS ,                   0000194501792002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000194601793002  
                NUMDIAC1+1             FOR 2 DIGITS ,                   0000194701794002  
                SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,");";                             0000194801795002  
    PROCESS EJECUTA(PARAM)[STAT_P015[NUMDIAC1]];                        0000194901796002  
    WAIT((3))                                  ;                        0000195001796102  
    INTENTOS := 0;                                                      0000195101796202  
    WHILE STATUS_MIX_P015(NUMDIAC1) = 0 AND                             0000195201796302  
          INTENTOS < 30                 DO                              0000195301796402  
    BEGIN                                                               0000195401796502  
       WAIT((.3))        ;                                              0000195501796602  
       INTENTOS := * + 1 ;                                              0000195601796702  
    END;                                                                0000195701796802  
    REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                                  0000195801798002  
    REPLACE ARR1 BY "REMOVE ",                                          0000195901799002  
                    "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P015/ASINCS",                    0000196001800002  
                      SIST_LIB          FOR 3 DIGITS,                   0000196101801002  
                      FEC_DIA[NUMDIAC1] FOR 6 DIGITS,                   0000196201802002  
                      "/",                                              0000196301803002  
                      MTP_P015 FOR 8,                                   0000196401804002  
                      " ON CMEMP"                                       0000196501805002  
                      48"00";                                           0000196601806002  
    DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                        0000196701807002  
 END OF EXTERNO_P015;                                                   0000196801808002

 BOOLEAN PROCEDURE EXTERNO_P025;                                        0000199801811002  
%*******************************************************************    0000199901812002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000200001813002  
    STAT_P025.STATUS := VALUE(NEVERUSED);                               0000200101814002  
    REPLACE CTLVER_ID BY "S151P025ACTS";                                0000200201815002  
    IF DAME_TIT(CTLVER_ID,CTLVER_NOM) < 0  THEN                         0000200301816002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000200401817002  
       REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ);                            0000200501818002  
       REPLACE MSGLJ BY HDRMSG,"I>020901< LIBRARY REGISTRA, ",          0000200601819002  
               "ERROR EN CTLVERS, NO EXISTE IDENTIFICADOR DE ",         0000200701820002  
               "P025 ASINCRONO S151 (S151P025ACT)" ;                    0000200801821002  
       LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                 0000200901822002  
     REPLACE NOMP025 BY "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P025/ACTBDSDOMOV/01MTP   ";  0000201001823002  
    END                                                                 0000201101824002  
    ELSE                                                                0000201201825002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000201301826002  
       REPLACE NOMP025 BY CTLVER_NOM UNTIL = " ", " ";                  0000201401827002  
       POSICION := SIZE(NOMP025);                                       0000201501828002  
       IF POSICION > 16 THEN                                            0000201601829002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000201701830002  
          POINT_P025 := NOMP025[POSICION - 16];                         0000201801831002  
          REPLACE MTP_P025 BY POINT_P025 FOR 8;                         0000201901832002  
       END                                                              0000202001833002  
       ELSE                                                             0000202101834002  
          REPLACE MTP_P025 BY "01MTP   ";                               0000202201835002  
    END;                                                                0000202301836002  
    REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                                  0000202401837002  
    REPLACE ARR1 BY "COPY ", NOMP025 UNTIL = " " ,                      0000202501838002  
                    " AS ",                                             0000202601839002  
                    "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P025/ACTBDS",                    0000202701840002  
                      SIST_LIB          FOR 3 DIGITS,                   0000202801841002  
                      "/",                                              0000202901842002  
                      MTP_P025 FOR 8,                                   0000203001843002  
                      " FROM CMEMP(PACK) TO CMEMP(PACK)",               0000203101844002  
                      48"00";                                           0000203201845002  
    DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                        0000203301846002  
    REPLACE NOM_ASINC BY "(S151)S151/OBJECT/P025/ACTBDS",               0000203401847002  
                          SIST_LIB          FOR 3 DIGITS,               0000203501848002  
                          "/",                                          0000203601849002  
                          MTP_P025 FOR 8,                               0000203701850002  
                          " ON CMEMP.",                                 0000203801851002  
                          48"00";                                       0000203901852002  
    REPLACE STAT_P025.NAME BY NOM_ASINC;                                0000204001853002  
    REPLACE PARAM BY SIST_LIB          FOR 3 DIGITS ;                   0000204101854002  
    PROCESS EJECUTA(PARAM)[STAT_P025];                                  0000204201855002  
    WAIT((3));                                                          0000204301856002  
 END OF EXTERNO_P025;                                                   0000204401857002

   BEGIN                                                                0000241202064002

                                                                        0000243102083002  
 END;                                                                   0000243202084002

               REPLACE CTLVER_ID BY "S151LOTE    ";                     0000259502193002

               ELSE                                                     0000260502203002

            END;                                                        0000263102218002  
            FINAL;                                                      0000263202219002  
         END;                                                           0000263302220002  
    ELSE:RESULT:= 2;                                                    0000263402221002  
    END;                                                                0000263502222002  
    REPLACE HHMMSS BY TIME(9) FOR 6   ;                                 0000263602222102  
    HORA := INTEGER(POINTER(HHMMSS),6);                                 0000263702222202  
    IF RESULT = 0 THEN                                                  0000263802223002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000263902224002  
       IF FUNCIONVAL  < 98                  AND                         0000264002224012  
          (STATUS_MIX_P015(NUMDIAC1)<1  OR                              0000264102224014  
          (STATUS_MIX_P016(NUMDIAC1)<1 AND HORA < 200000)) AND          0000264202224022

          IDFTIPPROC(ALOG1) < 16            AND                         0000264502226012  
          LEVANTA_P015                      THEN                        0000264602227002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000264702227004  
          CLOSE (LOGS[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                  0000264802267162  
          CLOSE (DESS[NUMDIAC1],LOCK);                                  0000264902267172

             (STATUS_MIX_P016(NUMDIAC1) < 1 AND                         0000265402267402  
              HORA < 200000)                THEN                        0000265502267502  
          BEGIN                                                         0000265602267602  
             VERSION_P015;                                              0000265702267702  
             VERSION_P016;                                              0000265802267802  
             WAIT((3));                                                 0000265902267902  
             EXTERNO_P015;                                              0000266002268002  
             EXTERNO_P016;                                              0000266102268102  
          END                                                           0000266202268202  
          ELSE                                                          0000266302268302  
             IF STATUS_MIX_P015(NUMDIAC1)  < 1 THEN                     0000266402268402  
             BEGIN                                                      0000266502268502  
                VERSION_P015;                                           0000266602268602  
                WAIT((3));                                              0000266702268702  
                EXTERNO_P015;                                           0000266802268802  
             END                                                        0000266902268902

       IF STATUS_MIX_P025 < 1 AND FEC_DIA[NUMDIAC1] = FECS151           0000267902269802

%      CAUSE (HAYMOV[NUMDIAC1]);                                        0000268402273002

       IF ACT_NIVEL = 1000                                              0000269302282002

    ELSE                                                                0000270002289002

       ACTNIVEL;                                                        0000270202291002

    END;                                                                0000270402293002  
 END;                                                                   0000270502294002  
 IF RESULT > 0 THEN                                                     0000270602295002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000270702296002

%====================   conversion de fechas                            0000279502381002  
  PROCEDURE a2ksdo_conv_amd_to_camd_hex (a2ksdo_date_six                0000279602382002  
                                        ,a2ksdo_date_eight);            0000279702383002  
  HEX ARRAY a2ksdo_date_six [0]                                         0000279802384002  
            ,a2ksdo_date_eight [0];                                     0000279902385002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000280002386002  
      INTEGER year, a2ksdo_base_year;                                   0000280102387002  
      POINTER a2ksdo_date_six_ptr,a2ksdo_date_eight_ptr;                0000280202388002  
      year := INTEGER (a2ksdo_date_six[0],2);                           0000280302389002  
      a2ksdo_date_six_ptr := a2ksdo_date_six [0] ;                      0000280402390002  
      a2ksdo_date_eight_ptr := a2ksdo_date_eight [0] ;                  0000280502391002  
      a2ksdo_base_year := 50;                                           0000280602392002  
      IF year geq a2ksdo_base_year THEN                                 0000280702393002  
          REPLACE a2ksdo_date_eight_ptr:a2ksdo_date_eight_ptr BY        0000280802394002  
              19 FOR 2 DIGITS                                           0000280902395002  
      ELSE                                                              0000281002396002  
          REPLACE a2ksdo_date_eight_ptr:a2ksdo_date_eight_ptr BY        0000281102397002  
              20 FOR 2 DIGITS;                                          0000281202398002  
                                                                        0000281302399002  
      REPLACE a2ksdo_date_eight_ptr BY a2ksdo_date_six_ptr for          0000281402400002  
          size(a2ksdo_date_six);                                        0000281502401002  
                                                                        0000281602402002  
      END;                                                              0000281702403002  
                                                                        0000281802404002  
%------------------------                                               0000281902412002  
% OUTER BLOCK OF CARGASDO                                               0000282002413002  
%------------------------                                               0000282102414002  
 RESULT_SDO:=0;                                                         0000282202415002  
 SIZESDOS1:=SIZE(DATOS);                                                0000282302416002  
%IF SIZESDOS1 > 180 THEN                                                0000282402417002  
%   SIZESDOS1:= 180;                                                    0000282502418002  
%REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1) BY 48"00"  FOR 180;                             0000282602419002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000282702420002  
% IF SIZESDOS1 > 216 THEN                                               0000282802421002  
%    SIZESDOS1:= 216;                                                   0000282902422002  
% REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1) BY 48"00"  FOR 216;                            0000283002423002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000283102424002  
%REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1) BY DATOS FOR SIZESDOS1;                         0000283202425002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000283302426002  
%INICIO CJMG CAMBIO PARA PROYECTO TESOFE 2019                           0000283402426102  
  IF SIZESDOS1 > 390 THEN                                               0000283502426202  
    SIZESDOS1:= 390;                                                    0000283602426302  
 REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1) BY 48"00"  FOR 390;                             0000283702426402  
%FIN CJMG CAMBIO PARA PROYECTO TESOFE 2019                              0000283802426502  
$SET OMIT = OLDCODE                                                     0000283902427002

$POP OMIT                                                               0000284102429002  
$SET OMIT = NOT OLDCODE                                                 0000284202430002  
 REPLACE POINTER(A2K_DATOS) BY 48"00"  FOR 216;                         0000284302431002

 REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1,4) BY POINTER(A2K_DATOS,4) FOR 36;               0000284502433002  
 IF IDCSDOFECPR(A2K_DATOS) > 0 THEN                                     0000284602434002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000284702435002  
    REPLACE A2K_HEX_6 BY POINTER(A2K_DATOS,4)+36 FOR 6;                 0000284802436002  
    A2KSDO_CONV_AMD_TO_CAMD_HEX(A2K_HEX_6, A2K_HEX_8);                  0000284902437002  
    REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1,4)+36 BY A2K_HEX_8[0] FOR 8;                  0000285002438002  
 END                                                                    0000285102439002  
 ELSE                                                                   0000285202440002  
    REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1,4)+36 BY 0 FOR 8 DIGITS;                      0000285302441002  
 IF IDCSDOFECIN(A2K_DATOS) > 0 THEN                                     0000285402442002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000285502443002  
    REPLACE A2K_HEX_6 BY POINTER(A2K_DATOS,4)+42 FOR 6;                 0000285602444002  
    A2KSDO_CONV_AMD_TO_CAMD_HEX(A2K_HEX_6, A2K_HEX_8);                  0000285702445002  
    REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1,4)+44 BY A2K_HEX_8[0] FOR 8;                  0000285802446002  
 END                                                                    0000285902447002  
 ELSE                                                                   0000286002448002  
    REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1,4)+44 BY 0 FOR 8 DIGITS;                      0000286102449002  
 IF IDCSDOFECFI(A2K_DATOS) > 0 THEN                                     0000286202450002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000286302451002  
    REPLACE A2K_HEX_6 BY POINTER(A2K_DATOS,4)+48 FOR 6;                 0000286402452002  
    A2KSDO_CONV_AMD_TO_CAMD_HEX(A2K_HEX_6, A2K_HEX_8);                  0000286502453002  
    REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1,4)+52 BY A2K_HEX_8[0] FOR 8;                  0000286602454002  
 END                                                                    0000286702455002  
 ELSE                                                                   0000286802456002  
   REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1,4)+52 BY 0 FOR 8 DIGITS;                       0000286902457002  
 IF IDCSDOFECUL(A2K_DATOS) > 0 THEN                                     0000287002458002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000287102459002  
    REPLACE A2K_HEX_6 BY POINTER(A2K_DATOS,4)+54 FOR 6;                 0000287202460002  
    A2KSDO_CONV_AMD_TO_CAMD_HEX(A2K_HEX_6, A2K_HEX_8);                  0000287302461002  
    REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1,4)+60 BY A2K_HEX_8[0] FOR 8;                  0000287402462002  
 END                                                                    0000287502463002  
 ELSE                                                                   0000287602464002  
    REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1,4)+60 BY 0 FOR 8 DIGITS;                      0000287702465002

 BEGIN                                                                  0000288002468002

   (IDFSDOINST(A2K_DATOS) = 15 OR IDFSDOINST(A2K_DATOS) = 17) THEN      0000288802476002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000288902477002

                                (SIZESDOS1*2 - 80);                     0000290402492002  
%INICIO CJMG TESOFE 2019                                                0000290502492102  
 REPLACE POINTER(SDOEXT)    BY 48"00"  FOR 390;                         0000290602492202  
 REPLACE POINTER(SDOEXT)    BY POINTER(ASDO1) FOR SIZESDOS1;            0000290702492302  
 REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1)     BY SDOEXT FOR 165;                          0000290802492402  
 REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1)+165 BY 48"00"  FOR 170;                         0000290902492502  
 REPLACE POINTER(ASDO1)+335 BY POINTER(SDOEXT)+165 FOR 55;              0000291002492602  
%FIN CJMG TESOFE 2019                                                   0000291102492702  
$POP OMIT                                                               0000291202493002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000291302494002  
 J_J    := -1;                                                          0000291402495002  
 BOOL_J := FALSE ;                                                      0000291502496002  
 WHILE J_J:= * + 1 LEQ 9 AND NOT BOOL_J DO                              0000291602497002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000291702498002  
    IF IDFSDOFECPR(ASDO1) = FEC_DIA[J_J] THEN                           0000291802499002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000291902500002  
       NUM_FEC  := J_J ;                                                0000292002501002  
       BOOL_J   := TRUE;                                                0000292102502002  
    END;                                                                0000292202503002  
 END;                                                                   0000292302504002  
 IF NOT BOOL_J THEN                                                     0000292402505002  
    RESULT_SDO := 4                                                     0000292502506002  
 ELSE                                                                   0000292602507002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000292702508002  
    CASE IDFSDOFUNC(ASDO1) OF                                           0000292802509002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000292902510002  
       80:BEGIN                                                         0000293002511002  
             NIVSDO_A[NUM_FEC] := 0   ;                                 0000293102512002  
             CLOSE (SDOS[NUM_FEC]);                                     0000293202513002  
             SDOS[NUM_FEC].MYUSE:= 1   ;                                0000293302514002  
%INICIO CJMG CAMBIO PROYECTO TESOFE 2019                                0000293402514502  
%             SDOS[NUM_FEC].BLOCKSIZE := 10800;                         0000293502515002  
%FIN CJMG CAMBIO PROYECTO TESOFE 2019                                   0000293602515502  
             CARGAMEMSDO;                                               0000293702516002  
             CLOSE (SDOS[NUM_FEC],LOCK);                                0000293802517002  
             SDOS[NUM_FEC].MYUSE:= 3   ;                                0000293902518002  
          END;                                                          0000294002519002  
       81:CARGAMEMSDO;                                                  0000294102520002  
       82:BEGIN                                                         0000294202521002  
             CARGAMEMSDO;                                               0000294302522002  
             CLOSE (SDOS[NUM_FEC],LOCK);                                0000294402523002  
          END;                                                          0000294502524002  
       ELSE:RESULT_SDO:= 2;                                             0000294602525002  
    END;                                                                0000294702526002  
 END;                                                                   0000294802527002  
 IF RESULT_SDO > 0 THEN                                                 0000294902528002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000295002529002
