 INTEGER PROCEDURE CONTROLES(DATOS);                                    0000298302569002  
 EBCDIC ARRAY DATOS[*];                                                 0000298402570002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000298502571002  
%*************************************************************          0000298602572002  
  DEFINE                                                                0000298702573002  
                                                                        0000298802574002  
        FUNCION       = INTEGER(DATOS[0],4)   #,                        0000298902575002  
%       FECADMOV      = INTEGER(DATOS[4],6)   #,                        0000299002576002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000299102577002  
        FECADMOV      = INTEGER(DATOS[4],8)   #,                        0000299202578002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000299302579002  
        NUMLOG        = INTEGER(DATOS[4],2)   #;                        0000299402580002  
  INTEGER RESULT,I,J,K, TIME_FECHA;                                     0000299502581002  
                                                                        0000299602582002  
%----------------------                                                 0000299702583002  
% CONTROLES OUTER BLOCK                                                 0000299802584002  
%----------------------                                                 0000299902585002  
% ACQUIREL(E3,A3);                                                      0000300002586002  
  RESULT:=0;                                                            0000300102587002  
  CASE FUNCION OF                                                       0000300202588002  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000300302589002  
     1:                                                                 0000300402590002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000300502591002  
         REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ)  ;                        0000300602592002  
         REPLACE MSGLJ BY "S151/L002/050:I>050110<";                    0000300702593002  
         REPLACE TXTMSG BY "** NIVELES DE ACTUALIZACION EN S151 **",    0000300802594002  
                           "** FECHA DE CONSULTA: ",                    0000300902595002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000301002597002  
                           FECPROC FOR 8 DIGITS," **";                  0000301102598002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000301202599002  
         LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                               0000301302600002  
         K:=-1;                                                         0000301402601002  
         WHILE K:= * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                       0000301502602002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000301602603002  
            REPLACE TXTMSG BY "** FECHA ", K+1  FOR * DIGITS, " ",      0000301702604002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000301802606002  
                                  FEC_DIA[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS,             0000301902607002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000302002608002  
                    ", NIVEL = ", NIVLOG_A[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS, " **";     0000302102609002  
            LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                            0000302202610002  
         END;                                                           0000302302611002  
         RESULT:=0                                                      0000302402612002  
       END;                                                             0000302502613002  
                                                                        0000302602614002  
     2:                                                                 0000302702615002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000302802616002  
         REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ)  ;                        0000302902617002  
         REPLACE MSGLJ BY "S151/L002/050:I>050110<";                    0000303002618002  
         REPLACE TXTMSG BY "** NIVELES DE ACTUALIZACION EN FILE **",    0000303102619002  
                           "** DESCRIPCIONES    : ";                    0000303202620002  
         LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                               0000303302621002  
         K:=-1;                                                         0000303402622002  
         WHILE K:= * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                       0000303502623002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000303602624002  
            REPLACE TXTMSG BY "** FECHA ", K+1  FOR * DIGITS, " ",      0000303702625002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000303802627002  
                                  FEC_DIA[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS,             0000303902628002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000304002629002  
                    ", NIVEL = ", NIVDES_A[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS, " **";     0000304102630002  
            LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                            0000304202631002  
         END;                                                           0000304302632002  
         RESULT:=0                                                      0000304402633002  
       END;                                                             0000304502634002  
     3:                                                                 0000304602635002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000304702636002  
         REPLACE MSGLJ BY " " FOR SIZE(MSGLJ)  ;                        0000304802637002  
         REPLACE MSGLJ BY "S151/L002/050:I>050110<";                    0000304902638002  
         REPLACE TXTMSG BY "** NIVELES DE ACT. EN S151 ",               0000305002639002  
                           "FECHA CONS : ",                             0000305102640002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000305202642002  
                           FECPROC FOR 8 DIGITS;                        0000305302643002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000305402644002  
         LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                               0000305502645002  
         K:=-1;                                                         0000305602646002  
         WHILE K:= * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                       0000305702647002  
         BEGIN                                                          0000305802648002  
            REPLACE TXTMSG BY " FECHA ", K+1  FOR * DIGITS, " ",        0000305902649002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000306002651002  
                                  FEC_DIA[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS,             0000306102652002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000306202653002  
                    ", NIVEL = ", NIVLOG_A[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS;            0000306302654002  
            LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                            0000306402655002  
            IF FEC_DIA[K] = FECPROC THEN                                0000306502656002  
               IF STATUS_MIX_P015(K) > 0 THEN                           0000306602657002  
               BEGIN                                                    0000306702658002  
                  REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                    0000306802659002  
                  REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P015(K) FOR 7 DIGITS,         0000306902660002  
                                  " HI 20 ",                            0000307002661002  
                                    48"00";                             0000307102662002  
                  DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                          0000307202663002  
               END;                                                     0000307302664002  
         END;                                                           0000307402665002  
         RESULT:=0                                                      0000307502666002  
       END;                                                             0000307602667002  
    5 : BEGIN                                                           0000307702667052  
           REPLACE DATOS[4] BY 0 FOR 18 DIGITS;                         0000307802667102  
           K := -1;                                                     0000307902667152  
           WHILE K:= * + 1 LEQ 9 DO                                     0000308002667202  
              IF FECPROC = FEC_DIA[K] THEN                              0000308102667252  
              BEGIN                                                     0000308202667302  
                 REPLACE DATOS[4] BY  K + 1        FOR 2 DIGITS,        0000308302667352  
                                      FECPROC      FOR 8 DIGITS,        0000308402667402  
                                      NIVLOG_A[K]  FOR 8 DIGITS;        0000308502667452  
                 K:= 10;                                                0000308602667502  
              END;                                                      0000308702667552  
        END;                                                            0000308802667602  
                                                                        0000308902667652  
    10:CIERRALOG(0);                                                    0000309002668002

    12:CIERRALOG(2);                                                    0000309202670002

    17:CIERRALOG(7);                                                    0000309702675002  
    18:CIERRALOG(8);                                                    0000309802676002  
    19:CIERRALOG(9);                                                    0000309902677002  
    22:BEGIN                                                            0000310002678002  
          L:= -1;                                                       0000310102679002  
          WHILE L:=*+1 LEQ 9 DO                                         0000310202680002  
          BEGIN                                                         0000310302681002  
             CIERRALOG(L);                                              0000310402682002  
             IF STATUS_MIX_P015(L) > 0 THEN                             0000310502683002  
             BEGIN                                                      0000310602684002  
                REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                      0000310702685002  
                REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P015(L) FOR 7 DIGITS, " HI 4",  0000310802686002  
                                  48"00";                               0000310902687002  
                DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                            0000311002688002  
             END;                                                       0000311102689002  
             IF STATUS_MIX_P016(L) > 0 THEN                             0000311202689012  
             BEGIN                                                      0000311302689022  
                REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                      0000311402689032  
                REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P016(L) FOR 7 DIGITS, " HI 4",  0000311502689042  
                                  48"00";                               0000311602689052  
                DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                            0000311702689062  
             END;                                                       0000311802689072  
          END;                                                          0000311902690002  
          CLOSE (ERRORES1,LOCK);                                        0000312002691002  
%         NIVERROR1 := 0;                                               0000312102692002  
          WAIT((5));                                                    0000312202693002  
          LEVANTA_P015 := FALSE;                                        0000312302694002  
       END;                                                             0000312402695002  
                                                                        0000312502696002  
    23: LEVANTA_P015 := TRUE;                                           0000312602697002  
    25:BEGIN                                                            0000312702698002  
          ACQUIREL(CAMBIA_FECHA);                                       0000312802699002  
          L:= -1;                                                       0000312902700002

    END;                                                                0000319502770002  
    50: ACQUIREL(CAMBIA_FECHA);                                         0000319602771002

       END;                                                             0000321602791002

    PTR_HEX := TEMP_ENT[162];                                           0000366003146002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000366103147002  
%   PTR_HEX := TEMP_ENT[164];                                           0000366203148002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000366303149002  
    PTR_EBC := PAR_ENT[6];                                              0000366403150002  
    FECINI:= IDFSALFECONS(REG);                                         0000366503151002  
    NIVINI:= IDFSALNIVINI(REG);                                         0000366603152002  
    NAT   := IDFSALNAT028(REG);                                         0000366703153002  
    INDS250 := IDFSALNAT250(REG);                                       0000366803154002  
    REPLACE PTR_HEX:PTR_HEX BY FECINI FOR 6 DIGITS,NIVINI FOR 2 DIGITS; 0000366903155002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000367003156002  
%   REPLACE PTR_HEX:PTR_HEX BY FECINI FOR 8 DIGITS,NIVINI FOR 2 DIGITS; 0000367103157002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000367203158002  
    IF FORMATO = 2 THEN                                                 0000367303159002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000367403160002  
      CLAVETR:=IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,2)                                      0000367503161002  
    END                                                                 0000367603162002  
    ELSE                                                                0000367703163002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000367803164002  
       CLAVETR:=IDFSALCVETRA(REG);                                      0000367903165002  
    END;                                                                0000368003166002  
    PTR_EBC := PAR_ENT[6];                                              0000368103167002  
    REPLACE PTR_EBC:PTR_EBC BY DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,1) FOR 12,               0000368203168002  
                               DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,2) FOR 12,               0000368303169002  
                               DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,3) FOR 12,               0000368403170002  
                               DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,4) FOR 12,               0000368503171002  
                               DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,5) FOR 12,               0000368603172002  
                               DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,6) FOR 12;               0000368703173002  
    IF RESULTADO > 1 AND NOT BOOL_CAT THEN                              0000368803174002  
    BEGIN                                                               0000368903175002  
       REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR LIB/REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,   0000369003176002  
                         ", CATALOGO S080 VACIO, ",                     0000369103177002  
                         " NO EXISTEN CLAVES DE TRANSACCION PARA S",    0000369203178002  
                         SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS;                         0000369303179002  
       LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                 0000369403180002  
    END;                                                                0000369503181002  
    IF RESULTADO = 0 THEN RESULTADO:=3;                                 0000369603182002  
    BOOL_CAT:=TRUE;                                                     0000369703183002  
    IF NAT = 1 OR NAT = 2 THEN                                          0000369803184002  
       IF INDS250 = 2 THEN                                              0000369903185002  
          REPLACE ADFCVEINDIC(CLAVETR) BY 3   FOR 1 DIGITS              0000370003186002  
       ELSE                                                             0000370103187002  
          REPLACE ADFCVEINDIC(CLAVETR) BY 1   FOR 1 DIGITS              0000370203188002  
    ELSE                                                                0000370303189002  
       IF INDS250 = 2 THEN                                              0000370403190002  
          REPLACE ADFCVEINDIC(CLAVETR) BY 2   FOR 1 DIGITS;             0000370503191002  
                                                                        0000370603192002  
    REPLACE ADFCVECOL  (CLAVETR) BY NAT FOR 1 DIGITS;                   0000370703193002  
 END;                                                                   0000370803194002  
 END OF CARGACVES;                                                      0000370903195002  
%HSC ALTA DISPONIBILIDAD IBM 10/02/2015                                 0000371003195012  
%----------------------------------------------                         0000371103195022  
 PROCEDURE VERIFICA_HA;                                                 0000371203195032  
%----------------------------------------------                         0000371303195042  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000371403195052  
                                                                        0000371503195062  
   REPLACE NOM_FIN500 BY "(S151)S151/FILE/FINS500/",                    0000371603195072  
                          FECHA_P150   FOR 8 DIGITS,                    0000371703195082  
                          " ON CMEMP."             ;                    0000371803195092  
   REPLACE FIN500.TITLE BY NOM_FIN500;                                  0000371903195102  
                                                                        0000372003195112  
   REPLACE NOM_FIN408 BY "(S151)S151/FILE/FINS408/",                    0000372103195122  
                          FECHA_P150   FOR 8 DIGITS,                    0000372203195132  
                          " ON CMEMP."             ;                    0000372303195142  
   REPLACE FIN408.TITLE BY NOM_FIN408;                                  0000372403195152  
                                                                        0000372503195162  
   IF FIN500.RESIDENT THEN                                              0000372603195164  
   BEGIN                                                                0000372703195166  
      FIN_S500 := TRUE;                                                 0000372803195168  
   END;                                                                 0000372903195170  
                                                                        0000373003195172  
   IF FIN408.RESIDENT THEN                                              0000373103195174  
   BEGIN                                                                0000373203195176  
      FIN_S408 := TRUE;                                                 0000373303195178  
   END;                                                                 0000373403195180  
                                                                        0000373503195182  
   IF FIN_S500 AND FIN_S408 THEN                                        0000373603195184  
   BEGIN                                                                0000373703195186  
      EJECUTA_P150;                                                     0000373803195190  
      FIN_S500 := FIN_S408 := FALSE;                                    0000373903195192  
   END;                                                                 0000374003195198  
                                                                        0000374103195222  
 END OF VERIFICA_HA;                                                    0000374203195232

%******************                                                     0000374503197002  
 PROCEDURE LEVANTA;                                                     0000374603198002  
%******************                                                     0000374703199002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000374803200002  
   DEFINE HFUNCION    = INTEGER(POINTER(ALOG,4),2)#,                    0000374903201002  
                                                                        0000375003202002  
%         HFECLOG     = INTEGER((POINTER(ALOG)+2),6)#,                  0000375103203002  
%         HNIVLOG     = INTEGER((POINTER(ALOG)+8),8)#;                  0000375203204002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000375303205002  
          HFECLOG     = INTEGER((POINTER(ALOG)+2),8)#,                  0000375403206002  
          HNIVLOG     = INTEGER((POINTER(ALOG)+10),8)#;                 0000375503207002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000375603208002  
%  ARRAY   ALOG,VACIO,ASDO[0:59],ACONTROLE[0:280];                      0000375703209002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000375803210002  
   ARRAY   ALOG[0:59];                                                  0000375903211002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000376003212002  
   INTEGER I,J,RESULT,RESULT_CTL,K,INT;                                 0000376103213002  
   INTEGER ARRAY NIVEL_K[0:9];                                          0000376203214002  
   INTEGER NIV_INF, NIV_SUP,NUM_REG;                                    0000376303215002  
                                                                        0000376403216002  
%----------------------------------------------                         0000376503217002  
 PROCEDURE ARMALOG(LOGN,NIVLOG,FECDIA,NIVBASE);                         0000376603218002  
%----------------------------------------------                         0000376703219002  
 VALUE LOGN,FECDIA,NIVBASE;                                             0000376803220002  
 INTEGER LOGN,NIVLOG,FECDIA,NIVBASE;                                    0000376903221002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000377003222002  
   LABEL VERIFICA;                                                      0000377103223002  
                                                                        0000377203224002  
   INTEGER NIVLOG2,I;                                                   0000377303225002  
                                                                        0000377403226002  
   NIVLOG:= 0;                                                          0000377503227002  
   VERIFICA:                                                            0000377603228002  
                                                                        0000377703229002  
                                                                        0000377803230002  
   REPLACE NOM_ARCMOV BY ARC_MOV FOR 34,                                0000377903231002  
                         FECDIA  FOR 6 DIGITS,                          0000378003232002  
                         " ON "              ,                          0000378103233002  
                         PK_MOV  FOR 17,"."  ;                          0000378203234002  
   IF LOGS[LOGN].OPEN THEN                                              0000378303235002  
      IF LOGS[LOGN].RESIDENT THEN                                       0000378403235012  
         CLOSE(LOGS[LOGN])                                              0000378503235022  
      ELSE                                                              0000378603235032  
      BEGIN                                                             0000378703235042  
         CLOSE(LOGS[LOGN],LOCK);                                        0000378803235052  
         OPEN (LOGS[LOGN],WAIT);                                        0000378903235062  
         CLOSE(LOGS[LOGN])     ;                                        0000379003235072  
      END;                                                              0000379103235082  
   LOGS[LOGN].MYUSE:= 3;                                                0000379203235102  
   REPLACE LOGS[LOGN].TITLE BY NOM_ARCMOV;                              0000379303236002  
                                                                        0000379403239002  
   IF LOGS[LOGN].RESIDENT THEN                                          0000379503240002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000379603241002  
      READ(LOGS[LOGN][0],75,ALOG);                                      0000379703242002  
     LAST_RLOG[LOGN]:=LOGS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                            0000379803243002  
     IF HFUNCION NEQ 99 THEN                                            0000379903244002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000380003245002  
        WHILE HFECLOG NEQ FECDIA DO                                     0000380103246002  
        BEGIN                                                           0000380203247002  
          CLOSE(LOGS[LOGN],LOCK);                                       0000380303248002  
          REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR LIB/REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,0000380403249002  
                            ", FECHA HEADER FILE/MOVTOS: ",             0000380503250002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000380603252002  
                            POINTER(ALOG)+2 FOR 8,                      0000380703253002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000380803254002  
                            " FECHA CORRECTA: ",                        0000380903255002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000381003257002  
                            FECDIA FOR 8 DIGITS;                        0000381103258002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000381203259002  
          LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                              0000381303260002  
          ACCEPT(TXTMSG);                                               0000381403261002  
          LOGS[LOGN].MYUSE:= 3;                                         0000381503262002  
           READ(LOGS[LOGN][0],75,ALOG);                                 0000381603263002  
        END;                                                            0000381703264002  
        NIV_SUP:=LOGS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                                 0000381803265002  
     END                                                                0000381903266002  
     ELSE                                                               0000382003267002  
        NIV_SUP:=0;                                                     0000382103268002  
                                                                        0000382203269002  
     NIV_INF:=0;                                                        0000382303270002  
                                                                        0000382403271002  
     WHILE NIV_INF NEQ NIV_SUP DO                                       0000382503272002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000382603273002  
          NUM_REG:=INTEGERT((NIV_SUP - NIV_INF)/2) + NIV_INF;           0000382703274002  
           READ(LOGS[LOGN][NUM_REG],75,ALOG);                           0000382803275002  
                                                                        0000382903276002  
          IF HFUNCION = 99   THEN                                       0000383003277002  
             NIV_SUP := NUM_REG                                         0000383103278002  
          ELSE                                                          0000383203279002  
             NIV_INF := NUM_REG;                                        0000383303280002  
                                                                        0000383403281002  
          IF NIV_SUP - NIV_INF = 1 THEN                                 0000383503282002  
             NIV_SUP := NIV_INF;                                        0000383603283002  
                                                                        0000383703284002  
       END;                                                             0000383803285002  
     NIVLOG:=NIV_SUP;                                                   0000383903286002  
     J := NIVLOG;                                                       0000384003287002  
      READ(LOGS[LOGN][J:=*+1],75,ALOG);                                 0000384103288002  
     WHILE HFUNCION NEQ 99 DO                                           0000384203289002  
        IF READ(LOGS[LOGN][J:=*+1],75,ALOG) THEN                        0000384303290002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000384403291002  
         REGSIZE:= 75;                                                  0000384503295102  
         AMPL_REG := 20000;                                             0000384603295202  
         DFAMPLIAFILE(LOGS[LOGN],J,AMPL_REG,A_VACIO,REGSIZE);           0000384703296002  
          READ(LOGS[LOGN][0]);                                          0000384803297002  
         LAST_RLOG[LOGN]:=LOGS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                        0000384903298002  
       END;                                                             0000385003312002  
   END                                                                  0000385103313002  
   ELSE                                                                 0000385203314002  
   IF NIVBASE > 0 THEN                                                  0000385303315002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000385403316002  
                                                                        0000385503317002  
      REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR : FALTA ARCHIVO ",                       0000385603318002  
                        LOGS[LOGN].TITLE        ,                       0000385703319002  
                        ", FAVOR DE COPIARLO   ";                       0000385803320002  
                                                                        0000385903321002  
      LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                  0000386003322002  
      ACCEPT(TXTMSG);                                                   0000386103323002  
      GO VERIFICA;                                                      0000386203324002  
   END;                                                                 0000386303325002  
 END ARMALOG;                                                           0000386403326002  
                                                                        0000386503327002  
%----------------------------------------------                         0000386603328002  
 PROCEDURE ARMADES(LOGN,NIVDES,FECDIA,NIVSDO);                          0000386703329002  
%----------------------------------------------                         0000386803330002  
 VALUE   LOGN,FECDIA;                                                   0000386903331002  
 INTEGER LOGN,NIVDES,FECDIA,NIVSDO;                                     0000387003332002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000387103333002  
%  DEFINE IDFFECDES = INTEGER((POINTER(ALOG)+2) ,6)#,                   0000387203334002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000387303335002  
   DEFINE IDFFECDES = INTEGER((POINTER(ALOG)+2) ,8)#,                   0000387403336002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000387503337002  
                                                                        0000387603338002  
           DFFECDES =          POINTER(ALOG)+2     #,                   0000387703339002  
%         IDFSTADES = INTEGER((POINTER(ALOG)+16),2)#,                   0000387803340002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000387903341002  
          IDFSTADES = INTEGER((POINTER(ALOG)+18),2)#;                   0000388003342002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000388103343002  
   LABEL VERIFICA;                                                      0000388203344002  
   INTEGER I;                                                           0000388303345002  
                                                                        0000388403346002  
   NIVDES :=0;                                                          0000388503347002  
   VERIFICA:                                                            0000388603348002  
   REPLACE NOM_ARCDES BY ARC_DES FOR 34,                                0000388703349002  
                         FECDIA  FOR 6 DIGITS,                          0000388803350002  
                         " ON "              ,                          0000388903351002  
                         PK_DES  FOR 17,"."  ;                          0000389003352002  
   IF DESS[LOGN].OPEN THEN                                              0000389103353002  
      IF DESS[LOGN].RESIDENT THEN                                       0000389203353102  
         CLOSE(DESS[LOGN])                                              0000389303353202  
      ELSE                                                              0000389403353302  
      BEGIN                                                             0000389503353352  
         CLOSE(DESS[LOGN],LOCK);                                        0000389603353402  
         OPEN (DESS[LOGN],WAIT);                                        0000389703353422  
         CLOSE(DESS[LOGN]);                                             0000389803353442  
      END;                                                              0000389903353462  
                                                                        0000390003353502  
   REPLACE DESS[LOGN].TITLE BY NOM_ARCDES;                              0000390103354002  
   DESS[LOGN].MYUSE := 3       ;                                        0000390203355002  
   REPLACE NOM_ARCSDO BY ARC_SDO FOR 34,                                0000390303356002  
                         FECDIA  FOR 6 DIGITS,                          0000390403357002  
                         " ON "              ,                          0000390503358002  
                         PK_SDO  FOR 17,"."  ;                          0000390603359002  
                                                                        0000390703360002  
   IF SDOS[LOGN].OPEN THEN                                              0000390803360102  
      IF SDOS[LOGN].RESIDENT THEN                                       0000390903360202  
         CLOSE(SDOS[LOGN])                                              0000391003360302  
      ELSE                                                              0000391103360402  
      BEGIN                                                             0000391203360502  
         CLOSE(SDOS[LOGN],LOCK);                                        0000391303360602  
         OPEN (SDOS[LOGN],WAIT);                                        0000391403360702  
         CLOSE(SDOS[LOGN])     ;                                        0000391503360802  
      END;                                                              0000391603360902  
   REPLACE SDOS[LOGN].TITLE BY NOM_ARCSDO;                              0000391703361002  
                                                                        0000391803364002  
%  IF SDOS[LOGN].RESIDENT THEN                                          0000391903365002  
%  BEGIN                                                                0000392003366002  
%     SDOS[LOGN].MYUSE := 3;                                            0000392103367002  
%     READ(SDOS[LOGN][0],30,ASDO);                                      0000392203368002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000392303369002  
%     READ(SDOS[LOGN][0],31,ASDO);                                      0000392403370002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000392503371002  
%     WHILE IDFFECSDO NEQ FECDIA DO                                     0000392603372002  
%     BEGIN                                                             0000392703373002  
%        CLOSE(SDOS[LOGN],LOCK);                                        0000392803374002  
%        ERRORMSG;                                                      0000392903375002  
%        REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR LIB/REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS, 0000393003376002  
%                                                                       0000393103377002  
%                          ", FECHA HEADER FILE/SDO : ",                0000393203378002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000393303380002  
%                          DFFECSDO   FOR 8,                            0000393403381002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000393503382002  
%                        " FECHA CORRECTA : ",                          0000393603383002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000393703385002  
%                        FECDIA     FOR 8 DIGITS;                       0000393803386002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000393903387002  
%      LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                 0000394003388002  
%      ACCEPT(TXTMSG);                                                  0000394103389002  
%      SDOS[LOGN].MYUSE:= 3;                                            0000394203390002  
%      READ(SDOS[LOGN][0],30,ASDO);                                     0000394303391002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000394403392002  
%      READ(SDOS[LOGN][0],31,ASDO);                                     0000394503393002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000394603394002  
%    END;                                                               0000394703395002  
%    NIVSDO:=SDOS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                                     0000394803396002  
%  END                                                                  0000394903397002  
%  ELSE                                                                 0000395003398002  
%  BEGIN                                                                0000395103399002  
%    SDOS[LOGN].MYUSE := 1 ;                                            0000395203400002  
%    NIVSDO:= 0;                                                        0000395303401002  
%  END;                                                                 0000395403402002  
   NUM_REG:=0;                                                          0000395503403002  
                                                                        0000395603405002  
   IF DESS[LOGN].RESIDENT THEN                                          0000395703406002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000395803407002  
     READ(DESS[LOGN][0],90,ALOG);                                       0000395903408002  
     LAST_RDES[LOGN]:=DESS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                            0000396003409002  
     IF HFUNCION  NEQ 99 THEN                                           0000396103410002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000396203411002  
       WHILE HFECLOG NEQ FECDIA DO                                      0000396303412002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000396403413002  
         CLOSE(DESS[LOGN],LOCK);                                        0000396503414002  
         REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR LIB/REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS, 0000396603415002  
                         ", FECHA HEADER FILE/DES : ",                  0000396703416002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000396803418002  
                         DFFECDES   FOR 8,                              0000396903419002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000397003420002  
                         " FECHA CORRECTA : ",                          0000397103421002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000397203423002  
                         FECDIA     FOR 8 DIGITS;                       0000397303424002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000397403425002  
         LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                               0000397503426002  
         ACCEPT(TXTMSG);                                                0000397603427002  
         DESS[LOGN].MYUSE:= 3;                                          0000397703428002  
         READ(DESS[LOGN][0],90,ALOG);                                   0000397803429002  
       END;                                                             0000397903430002  
       NIV_SUP:=DESS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                                  0000398003431002  
     END                                                                0000398103432002  
     ELSE                                                               0000398203433002  
        NIV_SUP:=0;                                                     0000398303434002  
                                                                        0000398403435002  
     NIV_INF:=0;                                                        0000398503436002  
                                                                        0000398603437002  
     WHILE NIV_INF NEQ NIV_SUP DO                                       0000398703438002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000398803439002  
          NUM_REG:=INTEGERT((NIV_SUP - NIV_INF)/2) + NIV_INF;           0000398903440002  
          READ(DESS[LOGN][NUM_REG],90,ALOG);                            0000399003441002  
                                                                        0000399103442002  
          IF HFUNCION = 99   THEN                                       0000399203443002  
             NIV_SUP := NUM_REG                                         0000399303444002  
          ELSE                                                          0000399403445002  
             NIV_INF := NUM_REG;                                        0000399503446002  
                                                                        0000399603447002  
          IF NIV_SUP - NIV_INF = 1 THEN                                 0000399703448002  
             NIV_SUP := NIV_INF;                                        0000399803449002  
                                                                        0000399903450002  
       END;                                                             0000400003451002  
     NIVDES:=NIV_SUP;                                                   0000400103452002  
     J := NIVDES;                                                       0000400203453002  
     READ(DESS[LOGN][J:=*+1],90,ALOG);                                  0000400303454002  
     WHILE HFUNCION NEQ 99 DO                                           0000400403455002  
       IF READ(DESS[LOGN][J:=*+1],90,ALOG) THEN                         0000400503456002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000400603457002  
         REGSIZE:=90;                                                   0000400703461102  
         AMPL_REG := 5000;                                              0000400803461202  
         DFAMPLIAFILE(DESS[LOGN],J,AMPL_REG,A_VACIO,REGSIZE);           0000400903462002  
         READ(DESS[LOGN][0]);                                           0000401003463002  
         LAST_RDES[LOGN]:=DESS[LOGN].LASTRECORD;                        0000401103464002  
       END;                                                             0000401203478002  
   END                                                                  0000401303479002  
   ELSE                                                                 0000401403480002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000401503481002  
     IF LOGS[LOGN].RESIDENT THEN                                        0000401603482002  
     BEGIN                                                              0000401703483002  
        READ(LOGS[LOGN][0],75,ALOG);                                    0000401803484002  
       IF IDFSTADES = 1 THEN                                            0000401903485002  
       BEGIN                                                            0000402003486002  
         ERRORMSG;                                                      0000402103487002  
         REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR LIB/REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS, 0000402203488002  
                           ", FALTA ", DESS[LOGN].TITLE ,               0000402303489002  
                           ", FAVOR DE COPIARLO "      ,                0000402403490002  
%                          FECDIA FOR 6 DIGITS;                         0000402503491002  
%INICIA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                                0000402603492002  
                           FECDIA FOR 8 DIGITS;                         0000402703493002  
%TERMINA CODIGO DE RENOVACION CRONOS 2000                               0000402803494002  
         LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                               0000402903495002  
         ACCEPT(TXTMSG) ;                                               0000403003496002  
         GO VERIFICA;                                                   0000403103497002  
       END                                                              0000403203498002  
       ELSE                                                             0000403303499002  
          NIVDES:= 0;                                                   0000403403500002  
     END                                                                0000403503501002  
     ELSE                                                               0000403603502002  
        NIVDES:= 0;                                                     0000403703503002  
   END;                                                                 0000403803504002  
 END ARMADES;                                                           0000403903505002  
                                                                        0000404003506002

 PROCEDURE BAJA;                                                        0000429003806002  
%***************                                                        0000429103807002  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000429203808002  
   INTEGER I,J, RESULT_L001, ESTADO, MEZCLA;                            0000429303809002  
                                                                        0000429403810002  
   BOOLEAN BOOL_FIN;                                                    0000429503811002  
   LABEL REGRESA;                                                       0000429603812002  
                                                                        0000429703813002  
%  FINMOV1:= FINMOV2:=FINMOV3:= TRUE;                                   0000429803814002  
   CLOSE (ERRORES1,LOCK);                                               0000429903815002  
   I:= -1;                                                              0000430003816002  
   WHILE I:=*+1 LEQ 9 DO                                                0000430103817002  
         CIERRALOG(I);                                                  0000430203819002  
   REGRESA:                                                             0000430303822002  
   I:= -1;                                                              0000430403824002  
   WHILE I:=*+1 LEQ 9 DO                                                0000430503825002  
   BEGIN                                                                0000430603826002  
      IF STATUS_MIX_P015(I) > 0 THEN                                    0000430703827002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000430803828002  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                             0000430903833002  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P015(I) FOR 7 DIGITS, " HI 4",         0000431003834002  
                           48"00";                                      0000431103835002  
         DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                   0000431203836002  
         WAIT((2));                                                     0000431303837002  
      END;                                                              0000431403838002  
      IF STATUS_MIX_P016(I) > 0 THEN                                    0000431503838012  
      BEGIN                                                             0000431603838022  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                             0000431703838032  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P016(I) FOR 7 DIGITS, " HI 4",         0000431803838042  
                           48"00";                                      0000431903838052  
         DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                   0000432003838062  
         WAIT((2));                                                     0000432103838072  
      END;                                                              0000432203838082  
      IF STATUS_MIX_P025 > 0 THEN                                       0000432303839002  
      BEGIN                                                             0000432403840002  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                             0000432503845002  
         REPLACE ARR1 BY NUM_MIX_P025 FOR 7 DIGITS, " HI 4",            0000432603846002  
                           48"00";                                      0000432703847002  
         DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                   0000432803848002  
         WAIT((2));                                                     0000432903849002  
      END;                                                              0000433003850002  
   END;                                                                 0000433103851002  
   BOOL_FIN := FALSE;                                                   0000433203851012  
   I:= 0            ;                                                   0000433303851022  
   ESTADO := STATUS_MIX_P025    ;                                       0000433403851024  
   MEZCLA := NUM_MIX_P025       ;                                       0000433503851026  
   VALIDA_STATUS(ESTADO, MEZCLA);                                       0000433603851028  
   WHILE (NOT BOOL_FIN) AND (I LEQ 9) DO                                0000433703851032  
      IF STATUS_MIX_P015(I) > 0 OR                                      0000433803851042  
         STATUS_MIX_P016(I) > 0 OR                                      0000433903851052  
         STATUS_MIX_P025    > 0 THEN                                    0000434003851062  
      BEGIN                                                             0000434103851072  
         BOOL_FIN := TRUE;                                              0000434203851082  
         ESTADO := STATUS_MIX_P015(I) ;                                 0000434303851092  
         MEZCLA := NUM_MIX_P015(I)    ;                                 0000434403851102  
         VALIDA_STATUS(ESTADO, MEZCLA);                                 0000434503851112  
         ESTADO := STATUS_MIX_P016(I) ;                                 0000434603851122  
         MEZCLA := NUM_MIX_P016(I)    ;                                 0000434703851132  
         VALIDA_STATUS(ESTADO, MEZCLA);                                 0000434803851142  
      END                                                               0000434903851182  
      ELSE                                                              0000435003851192  
         I := * + 1;                                                    0000435103851202  
   IF BOOL_FIN THEN GO REGRESA;                                         0000435203851212  
   REPLACE ACONTROLE2 BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(ACONTROLE2);                   0000435303854002  
   REPLACE DFB05FUNCION(ACONTROLE2) BY    22 FOR 2 DIGITS,              0000435403855002  
                                    SIST_LIB FOR 4 DIGITS,              0000435503856002  
                                    0        FOR 8 DIGITS,              0000435603857002  
                                    2        FOR 4 DIGITS;              0000435703858002  
   RESULT_L001:= B05PROCESOS(ACONTROLE2);                               0000435803859002  
                                                                        0000435903875002  
 END OF BAJA;                                                           0000436003876002

 PROCEDURE PROC_CONTROL;                                                0000441203915102  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000441303915202  
   INTEGER EVENTO, NUM_HI, FECHA_CAT_1735;                              0000441403916002  
   EBCDIC ARRAY DAT_CONTROL[0:11];                                      0000441503917002  
   NUM_HI := 0;                                                         0000441603918002  
   WHILE MYSELF.LIBRARYUSERS > 0 DO                                     0000441703919002
